115 年 10 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
10 月出口 967.6 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 10.9%，較上
年同月增 58.9%(按新臺幣計算增 66.6%)；累 計 1 至 10 月出口 7,582.9
億美元，較上年同期增 47.7%(按新臺幣計算增 51.1%)。 
（二）進口 
10 月進口 688.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 8.3%，較上
年同月增 75.1%(按新臺幣計算增 83.5%)；累 計 1 至 10 月進口 5,697.6
億美元，較上年同期增 44.6%(按新臺幣計算增 47.6%)。 
（三）出入超 
10 月出超 278.8 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 63.3 億美元；
累計 1 至 10 月出超 1,885.4 億美元，較上年同期增 689.7 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續拓展，
加上適逢傳統外銷旺季，以及價格攀升效應，10 月出口較上年同月
增 358.7 億美元(+58.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 476.0 億美元(歷年單月新高)，較上年同月增
200.7 億美元(+72.9%)，其中電腦及其附屬單元增 151.6 億美元
(+67.5%)、電腦之零附件增 18.5 億美元(+1.0 倍)、儲存媒體增 17.3
億美元(+2.1 倍)、交換器及路由器增 11.7 億美元(+1.3 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 326.8 億美元(歷年單月新高)，較上年同月增 124.3
億美元(+61.4%)，其中積體電路增 120.5 億美元(+63.0%)、印刷電
路增 1.9 億美元(+38.5%)、電容器及電阻器增 1.2 億美元(+48.6%)。 
3. 基本金屬及其製品出口 28.8 億美元，較上年同月增 7.6 億美元
(+35.7%)，其中銅及其製品增 4.7 億美元(+1.2 倍)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.9 億美元(+16.4%)。 
4. 機械出口 28.8 億美元(歷年單月新高)，較上年同月增 7.5 億美元
(+35.5%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.9 億美元(+57.5%)、機器、
工廠或實驗室設備增 1.4 億美元(+70.0%)。 
5. 礦產品出口 15.5 億美元，較上年同月增 6.5 億美元(+71.6%)，其
中石油煉製品增 5.7 億美元(+72.2%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月出口較上年同期增 2,448.3 億美元(+47.7%)，以資通
與視聽產品增 1,423.3 億美元(+72.5%)、電子零組件增 850.5 億美元
(+47.5%)增加較多，礦產品、機械亦各增 41.9 億美元(+36.8%)、41.5
億美元(+19.8%)。

生產等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口衍生需求之帶動，積體電路、資通產品
及半導體設備進口持續強勁，加上國際原物料行情居高，10 月總進
口較上年同月增 295.5 億美元(+75.1%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口 458.3 億美元，較上年同月增 195.0 億美元(+74.0%)，
其中電子零組件增 136.1 億美元(+1.1 倍)、礦產品增 22.8 億美元
(+58.0%)、電腦之零附件增 16.2 億美元(+70.0%)、基本金屬增 6.2
億美元(+33.4%)、塑化原料增 5.0 億美元(+22.0%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 163.4 億美元，較上年同月增 84.1 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通設備增 46.9 億美元(+2.0 倍)、半導體設備增 22.4 億美元
(+83.4%)、交通運輸設備增 7.6 億美元(+7.0 倍)。 
3. 消費品進口 61.1 億美元，較上年同月增 18.8 億美元(+44.5%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.0 億美元(+5.3 倍)、食品增 0.5 億
美元(+5.1%)、小客車增 0.4 億美元(+10.2%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
10 月對美國、歐洲、日本出口創新高 
1. 對美國出口 329.3 億美元，較上年同月增 118.1 億美元(+55.9%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 98.3 億美元(+56.6%)、電子零組件增 13.5
億美元(+1.7 倍)、機械增 2.4 億美元(+41.6%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 204.4 億美元，較上年同月增 61.4 億美元
(+42.9%)，其中電子零組件增 47.4 億美元(+48.0%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.6 億美元(+36.5%)、基本金屬及其製品增 3.2 億美元
(+95.2%)。 
3. 對東協出口 184.6 億美元，較上年同月增 84.1 億美元(+83.8%)，
其中電子零組件增 38.6 億美元(+75.5%)、資通與視聽產品增 36.5
億美元(+1.4 倍)、礦產品增 5.0 億美元(+2.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 59.5 億美元，較上年同月增 18.7 億美元(+46.0%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 10.7 億美元(+47.9%)、電子零組件增 3.3 億
美元(+81.3%)、機械增 1.2 億美元(+50.8%)。 
5. 對日本出口 36.7 億美元，較上年同月增 12.2 億美元(+49.6%)，其
中電子零組件增 7.5 億美元(+63.2%)、資通與視聽產品增 3.2 億美
元(+1.3 倍)、化學品增 0.5 億美元(+47.0%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月對美國出口 2,407.1 億美元，較上年同期增 891.1 億美
元(+58.8%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,805.3 億美元，增 416.6 億美
元(+30.0%)，對東協出口 1,524.1 億美元，增 547.6 億美元(+56.1%)，
對歐洲出口 481.3 億美元，增 157.1 億美元(+48.5%)，對日本出口 314.4
億美元，增 70.5 億美元(+28.9%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
10 月自東協進口 134.1 億美元，較上年同月增 82.3 億美元(+1.6 倍)，
自南韓進口 130.0 億 美 元，增 73.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)，自中國大陸與香
港進口 128.3 億美元，增 51.9 億美元(+67.9%)，自日本進口 62.0 億
美元，增 18.8 億美元(+43.4%)，自美國進口 53.6 億美元，增 14.7 億
美元(+37.9%)，自歐洲進口 48.2 億美元，增 9.5 億美元(+24.6%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較，自東協增 85.6%、
自南韓增 72.7%、自中國大陸與香港增 45.3%、自日本增 27.4%、自
美國增 23.0%、自歐洲增 12.1%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）今年 1-9 月主要國家/地區出口均呈上升，其中南韓(1-10 月)、香港、
新加坡受 AI 相關需求帶動科技供應鏈及區域貿易活動熱絡，累計出
口分別年增 60.6%、52.3%、41.3%；中國大陸、美國各年增 21.3%
及 14.3%，德國(1-8 月)、日本各增 8.8%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步成長，累計 1-9 月以香港進口年增 51.8%較
高，新加坡、中國大陸、南 韓 (1-10 月)分別年增 39.0%、27.5%及 21.4%，
德國(1-8 月)、日本、美國各增 8.4%、8.2%、6.7%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）隨全球人工智慧(AI)基礎建設投資強勁成長，帶動相關供應鏈需求擴
增，加以外銷產品價格攀升，10 月出口達 967.6 億美元，首度突破
900 億美元，年增 58.9%，連續 36 個月正成長；進口 688.9 億美元，
同為歷年單月最高，年增 75.1%，主要受惠於 AI 產業之國際分工與
出口引申需求增加，以及國際原物料行情高漲；出超 278.8 億美元亦
創新高。累計 1 至 10 月出口年增 47.7%、進口增 44.6%。
（二）10 月出口主要貨品中，資通與視聽產品、電子零組件出口值雙創單月
新高，各年增 72.9%及 61.4%，主因 AI、高效能運算及雲端服務等應
用商機熱絡；其餘貨類出口平均增 25.7%，其中礦產品因油價支撐增
71.6%，機械、電機產品受惠於半導體業者擴充產能及 AI 需求外溢，
增幅皆達 3 成，基本金屬及其製品亦增 35.7%。累 計 1 至 10 月資通與
視聽產品、電子零組件合占總出口比重約 8 成，年增 60.6%。

（三）10 月對主要市場出口年增率均逾 4 成，以對東協增 83.8%較突出，
對美國、日本、歐洲出口規模皆為單月新高，各增 55.9%、49.6%及
46.0%，對中國大陸與香港增 42.9%。累 計 1 至 10 月對五大市場出口
增幅介於 28%~59%，其中對美國出口年增 58.8%、對東協增 56.1%，
占總出口比重各為 31.7%及 20.1%，分別寫下近 36 年同期及歷年同
期新高紀錄。
（四）展望未來，全球經濟仍面臨地緣政治衝突、美國貿易政策走向等不
確定因素，惟隨人工智慧應用逐漸擴散至推論、代理式 AI，且國際
雲端服務業者持續布建 AI 基礎設施，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供
應鏈需求延續，加以科技新品相繼推 出、年底消費旺季來臨之挹注，
我國第 4 季出口可望維持強勁成長態勢。

### Page 2

足元の経済・金融状況と注目点
1
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 11.17
米国では物価高で実質賃金が伸び悩む一方、
カード延滞率に改善の兆し。NY連銀によれば、
延滞率の高止まりはコロナ期の与信拡大が影響
しており、今後も緩やかな改善が続くとの見立て
米家計のクレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
米国
10月の消費者物価は市場予想を上回る結果。生産
者物価も高止まり。市場は12月利上げを織り込み。
一方で、金利上昇を背景に中古住宅販売は大幅減。
根強いインフレ圧力と住宅市場の弱さが鮮明に
欧州
ユーロ圏の7～9月期GDPは前期から減速。ドイツ
は小幅プラスも、フランスはマイナス、スペインは堅
調と主要国でばらつき。ユーロ圏全体では雇用の伸
びも横ばいとなっており、景気回復力の鈍さを確認
中国
10月の輸出は高い伸びを維持。半導体や自動車な
どがけん引。一方で輸入も大幅増。生産は輸出堅調
で底堅く推移も、投資は下落幅が拡大し、消費も力
強さ欠き低迷。消費者物価も前月から伸びが鈍化

日本
9月の実質消費支出は10カ月連続のマイナス。景気
ウォッチャー調査も6カ月ぶりに悪化し、家計部門の
弱さを示唆する内容。国内企業物価は原油高などを
受けて再び加速。今後消費者物価へ波及する公算
注目点①
日本の消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な回復。
一方、大型M&Aなどから企業の資金需要は旺盛
で、銀行貸出は高い伸びを維持。預金獲得競争
が激化するなか、安定的な預金調達が重要に
日本の家計は消費に慎重、企業は借入に積極的
注目点②

米国：物価高で実質賃金は伸び悩むも、クレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
 2026年10月のコアCPI（食品・エネルギーを除く）は前月比＋0.3％と市場予想（同＋0.2％）を上回る結果
―3カ月移動平均では2025年1月以来の高水準。財・サービス価格ともに上昇が続きインフレ圧力の根強さを確認
― 名目賃金の増加が物価上昇に相殺され、実質平均時給は前月比横ばい。家計の購買力は伸び悩み
 一方、クレジットカードの90日以上延滞への新規移行率は高水準ながらも小幅に低下
―7～9月期は6.87％と前年同期の7.05％を下回り、家計の信用状況には改善の兆し
3
（出所）米国労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：カード延滞率の高さは足元の所得環境だけではなく、過去の与信拡大の影響を反映
 NY連銀によれば、コロナ禍には信用力の低い借り手へのカード発行が拡大
― 全体の信用スコアが改善する中、新規発行時の信用スコア中央値は低下。リスクの高い層への与信を増やした格好
 当時発行されたカードがその後の延滞率上昇に大きく寄与
― ただし、コロナ期の与信拡大による影響は徐々にはく落。加えて、2023年以降は新規発行時の信用力が改善しており、
雇用・所得環境が大きく悪化しなければ、今後も延滞率の緩やかな改善が続く公算

日本：個人消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な拡大。先行き不安が回復の重石に
5
 個人消費は拡大基調を維持も、所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な拡大
―9月の消費活動指数は前年比＋0.8％と拡大基調を維持したものの、耐久財特需のはく落などから増勢は鈍化
― 実質雇用者報酬が着実に増加する一方、消費の伸びは所得を下回る状況が継続
 先行き不安も家計の慎重な支出姿勢に影響
― 消費者マインドは食品やエネルギーなど身近な消費財の価格上昇に対する懸念から低調に推移
― 中東情勢を巡る不透明感もあり、当面の消費は緩慢な拡大にとどまる見通し

日本：大型M&Aなどもあり銀行貸出は高い伸び。金利上昇下でも企業の資金需要は底堅く推移
6
 企業の資金需要は旺盛。銀行貸出は高い伸びを維持
―10月の貸出残高（銀行・信用金庫計）は前年比＋5.2％と都市銀行を中心に高い伸び。とりわけ、大企業の資金需要が
旺盛。中堅・中小企業の資金需要にも底堅さ。活発な企業買収も資金需要の追い風に
 金利上昇下でも当面の資金需要は底堅い見通し
― 日銀の利上げに伴い新規貸出金利は上昇も、投資需要などを背景に企業の借入需要は底堅く推移する見込み
― なお、地域銀行を中心に預貸率が上昇。メガバンクを含め預金獲得競争が激化するなか、安定的な預金調達が重要に

（電力価格、2026年1～8月累積、前年同期比、％）
米国：民主党勝利によるデータセンター規制の強化は電力価格の上昇を抑制する可能性
 米国では家庭向け電力価格が上昇。背景に送配電設備の更新・増強や異常気象
― 足元では、データセンター（DC）稼働数と電力価格の上昇率に明確な関係はみられず
 一方、DC増設に伴う将来の電気料金負担増や水資源への影響を懸念し、規制強化を求める動きが拡大
― 中間選挙では、DC規制の強化を掲げる民主党知事候補が各州で勝利。とりわけ、DCの集積が進むテキサス・オハイ
オ・ジョージアでは、長年共和党が知事職を握ってきたものの、民主党候補が勝利する結果。今後、DC開発を巡る規制
が強化され、家計への費用転嫁が抑えられれば、中長期的な電力価格の上昇ペースは緩和される可能性
7
（注） 青色はオハイオ州、テキサス州、ジョージア州

主な方針州・当選者
条件を満たさないDCの建設を停止。 事業者
に電気・ガス・水道の費用を全額負担させ、

報公開、環境基準の遵守などを義務付ける

エイミー・ア
クトン氏
新規DCの承認を一時停止。 住民が建設の
是非に関与できる法律が整うまで、新規案件
を認めない。DC向けの税優遇を廃止し、送

ない方針
テキサス
ジーナ・ヒノ
ホサ氏
州全体で新規DC建設を一時停止。 電気料
金や水資源への影響を調査し、建設ルール
を整備。DC向けの税優遇を廃止し、電力設
備の整備費用を事業者に負担させる方針

22 23 24 25 26 （年）

欧州：ユーロ圏経済はプラス成長継続。一方、フランスでは不安定な政治情勢が投資の重石に
 ユーロ圏全体の7～9月期GDPは潜在成長率（約＋1.2％）を上回る伸びを維持
― アイルランドを除くと年率＋0.8％成長。スペインは内需主導で高成長維持も、フランスがマイナス転化する等国ごとに濃淡
 フランスの不安定な政治情勢が景気に暗雲。設備投資の下押し要因に
― フランスでは不安定な政治情勢が経済の不確実性を高めるなか、民間設備投資の足踏みが続く
― 同国の10月企業景況感は前月から大幅に悪化し、縮小圏に転落。先行きも、政治・財政の不透明感の長期化や金融環境
の引き締まりを背景に、一部業種（情報通信サービス等）を除き低調に推移する見込み
8
ユーロ圏：主要国の実質GDP成長率

（出所）Eurostatより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
フランス：不確実性指数と民間設備投資

― 先行きは、信用スプレッド拡大など金融環境のタイト化を通じたAI投資の選別・減速が、アジアの輸出拡大に波及するリ

### Page 14

日本：7～9月期実質GDPは横ばい圏の推移。消費低調の一方、設備投資が堅調に回復
 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）とほぼ横ばい
― 個人消費が2四半期連続でマイナスとなったほか、輸出が一時的要因もあり小幅減となり、成長率を下押し。一方、設備
投資はAI関連需要の堅調さを背景にプラス転換し、成長率を押し上げ
―4～6月期（前期比＋0.4％）から減速し、経済の緩やかな拡大傾向が一服
 今後、2026年度後半の実質GDPは、均してみれば小幅なプラス成長と予想
― 個人消費が精彩を欠く一方で、設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が継続。2026年度全体では＋1％弱の成長率に

Document 111: 2027“超级美元”？

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027“超级美元”？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

德意志银⾏上调 2027 年美元预测，认为 AI 热潮推⾼美债实际利率并吸引超万亿美元外资股
权流⼊，铸就了强势 “AI 美元 ” 。但美元⾼位⽀撑结构脆弱，过度依赖 AI 资本开⽀与 “ 芯⽚美
元 ” 循环，叠加美国双⾚字恶化，⼀旦 AI 预期逆转，美元将⾯临实质性下⾏⻛险。
摘要：
⼈⼯智能热潮重塑全球资⾦流向，美元⾼位徘徊，但脆弱的⽀撑结构令超级美元前景充满变
数。
据追⻛交易台消息，德意志银⾏在最新外汇展望报告中⼤幅上调 2027 年美元预测，承认此前对
美元⾛弱的判断落空。AI 热潮推⾼美国实际利率、吸引海量股权资⾦流⼊、重燃通胀压⼒并促使
美联储转向鹰派，共同铸就了当前的 "AI 美元 " 格局。美元已较过去四年均值⾼出逾 20% ，依据三
项基本⾯估值指标均居 G10 最贵货币之列。
德意志银⾏预计，美元将在 2027 年上半年延续强势，下半年出现温和回落，但整体仍将维持⾼
位。报告同时警告，美元对 AI 资本⽀出周期的依赖程度持续加深，⼀旦市场对 AI 回报预期出现重
⼤下调，美元两⼤⽀撑⽀柱 —— 美国实际利率与股权资⾦流⼊ —— 将同步⽡解，届时美元存在

实质性下⾏⻛险。
AI 热潮⽀撑 " 美元例外论 "
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

美元强势的核⼼驱动⼒源⾃美国国内，且短期内存在延续甚⾄强化的可能。过去数⽉美国通胀
重新加速，核⼼ PCE 平缩指数料将突破 3% ，推动利率市场重新定价，使美元重回 G10 前三⾼息
货币之列 —— 此前该地位曾于 2025 年底⼀度拱⼿相让。德意志银⾏数据显⽰，历史上美元提供
⾼息时鲜少进⼊下⾏周期。
实际收益率的上升同步提振了美国中性利率预期，今年上⾏约 50 个基点，⽽欧元区同期⼏乎未
变。与此同时，美国 GDP 增⻓预期重回 G10 前列，资本⽀出增速约为其他经济体两倍， AI 驱动
的产能扩张周期为⽣产率提升奠定基础，对汇市形成正向激励。
股权流⼊为主，固收资⾦现分流迹象
资⾦流向层⾯，美国仍是全球股权投资的核⼼磁极。外资流⼊美国股市规模突破每年 1 万亿美
元，成为美国外部⾚字融资的主要来源，且此类资⾦通常对冲⽐例较低，对美元的⽀撑效应更
为直接。
然⽽固收资⾦的流向正悄然⽣变。来⾃德意志银⾏的数据显⽰，美国债券资⾦流⼊有所放缓，
加拿⼤、⽇本等其他 G10 市场的债券资⾦流⼊则显著回升，动摇了 " 别⽆他选 " （ TINA ）的市场叙

事。外汇储备管理机构同样在过去⼀年半持续增加对其他⼩型货币的配置。这⼀趋势值得警惕
—— 若美元资产的分散化价值持续下降，对冲⽐率的上⾏压⼒将随之增加。
⾼估值与双⾚字构成中期隐患
美元的中期前景⾯临多重结构性压⼒。最受关注的是对 AI 资本⽀出周期的过度依赖 —— 过去三年
相关资本⽀出增⻓近五倍，但预计该势头对增⻓的拉动效应将在 2028 年后逐步消退。科技股的
波动迄今尚未传导⾄美元，但德意志银⾏认为，⼀旦 AI 资本⽀出预期出现更明显下调，美元将
⾯临实质冲击。
与此同时，美国财政⾚字稳定在约 GDP 的 6% ，经常账⼾⾚字也在重新扩⼤，逼近 GDP 的 4% ，
接近 G10 最差⽔平。从贸易加权实际汇率⻆度看，潜在贸易逆差已呈现⻓期趋势性恶化，仅因美
国能源收⽀由负转正⽽在总量数据中被掩盖。关税⻛险亦是隐患 —— 近年来历史经验显⽰，关
税威胁倾向于压低美元，⽽⾮推⾼。

### Page 3

亚洲货币：北亚资本重配牵动美元命运
北亚货币⾛势正成为美元前景中⽇益关键的变量。⽇元、韩元与新台币在美联储美元指数中的
合计权重已超过欧元，⽽这些经济体正积累着全球最⼤规模的贸易顺差，其顺差如何处置 ——
回流美国、另辟他途还是在国内转换 —— 将对美元⾛势产⽣决定性影响。
德意志银⾏将这⼀现象定义为 " 芯⽚美元 " 时代：芯⽚约占每 1 美元数据中⼼投资价值的 50% ，芯
⽚制造业集中在亚洲，产⽣的贸易顺差随后通过投资美国资本市场回流，形成⼀个⾃我强化的
循环，但也蕴含⼀旦 AI 重估便可能崩塌的系统性脆弱性。
具体⽽⾔，德意志银⾏预计美元兑⽇元将向 140 区间移动。⽇本央⾏加息、政府推动储蓄本⼟化
部署（包括有意推动 GPIF 将更多资产转向国内）可能引发资本回流。
欧元：法国政治⻛险压制，但改善可期
欧元短期内将持续承压，法国总统⼤选（⾸轮投票预计在 2027 年 4 ⽉）及随后的议会选举构成最
主要的不确定性来源，料将使 EUR/USD 在未来数⽉内维持 1.10 偏下⽅的下⾏⻛险。 ECB ⽬前市
场定价暗⽰加息 60 个基点以上，远超德意志银⾏预期的 25 个基点，亦对欧元形成额外拖累。
然⽽德意志银⾏认为，法国⼤选本⾝更可能成为市场出清事件⽽⾮⻛险放⼤器。法国政治⻛险
溢价已在市场充分反映，法国国债利差相对基本⾯已存在明显溢价；此外，除极左翼外，法国
各主要政治⼒量均以财政整顿为纲领，稳定政府预期下的分裂程度难以超越现状。企业利润趋
势向好、股权与债券资⾦流⼊双双扩⼤，均构成欧元中期回稳的基础。德意志银⾏对 EUR/USD
全年区间的判断约为 1.07 ⾄ 1.15 。
新兴市场：套息空间收窄，分化加剧
经历强劲⼀年后，新兴市场外汇的低垂果实已所剩⽆⼏。德意志银⾏对 CEEMEA 和拉美地区外
汇持更为审慎的态度，主因包括与美国的实际利差收窄、仓位偏重以及供给与财政⻛险上升。
具体⽽⾔，波兰兹罗提、捷克克朗和以⾊列新谢克尔⾯临较⼤的实际利差压缩压⼒；⼟⽿其⾥
拉、埃及镑和南⾮兰特则对供应冲击较为敏感。拉美⽅⾯，巴西雷亚尔因较⾼实际利率和强劲
贸易条件仍具⽀撑，但财政⻛险令前景趋于平衡；墨西哥⽐索因经济增⻓疲弱和央⾏偏鸽⽴场
继续承压；安第斯地区货币因贸易条件顺⻛、估值便宜及潜在改⾰红利⽽相对看好。边境市场
仍是亮点，但德意志银⾏已对哈萨克斯坦坚⼽和乌兹别克斯坦苏姆的看涨预期有所收敛。

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components

Solid 3Q26 results; positive tone to 4Q26/ 
2027; reiterate Buy; trim PO to NT$3,650

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,650 TWD | Price: 2,790 TWD

3Q26: record-high GM/OPM for five straight quarters 
Asia Vital Components (AVC)’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 33.9%/28.5% hit a record high for the 
fifth straight quarter, exceeding BofAe of 33.0%/27.5% and consensus of 33.3%/27.9%. 
OP (+25% QoQ, +108% YoY) was 7%/4% above BofAe/consensus, with higher sales 
(+20% QoQ, +52% YoY; 3%/2% above BofAe/consensus). We attribute the results beat 
to solid product mix (server and networking comprised 66% of 3Q26 sales, same as 1Q-
2Q26) and improving production efficiency. Despite a non-operating loss (mainly FX 
loss), NP was 10%/3% above consensus (Exhibit 3).  
Positive tone to 4Q26/2027; ongoing capacity expansion 
We expect 4Q26 sales to rise 19% QoQ and 2027 sales to grow 40% YoY, with mgmt.’s 
still positive tone to 4Q26/2027, mainly driven by AI liquid cooling projects. AVC 
indicated full support to clients’ platform upgrade and shipment pipeline. It should be 
able to pass through the higher raw material cost. In 2027, mgmt. targets ASIC sales mix 
to surpass GPU. AVC also sees solid sales momentum from chassis/rack and CDU, acting 
as the solution provider. Mgmt. maintained 2026 capex guidance at NT$15bn and guided 
NT$15-20bn for 2027 (BofAe unchanged at NT$17bn), mainly for capacity expansion in 
Vietnam. Mgmt. now targets another 50% cold plate capacity expansion in 2027. 
Trim PO to NT$3,650 for a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 3-10%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 results beat and higher 
margins, driven by ongoing server/networking project ramp-up. Yet, we cut our PO to 
NT$3,650 (23x 2027E EPS), with a lower P/E multiple (2SD above average since 2020; 
Exhibit 5) from NT$3,888 (was 27x; 3SD above) on AI supply chain’s potentially higher 
shipment/pipeline uncertainty due to component shortage. Nevertheless, we reiterate 
Buy on AVC as we believe it should remain one of the key beneficiaries from rising 
liquid-cooling adoption and ongoing GPU/ASIC platform/spec upgrade. We also highlight 
solid sales/margin growth under broad product base and diversified project/client base.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 8.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
AVC is the leading system-level thermal/cooling component 
maker globally. Key end markets include server, PC, 
smartphone. Key end clients include global CSPs, PC brands, 
smartphone brands. AVC was founded in 1991 and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange on 27 Sep 2002. It 
is headquartered in Taiwan. Its main production sites are 
located in China (Shenzhen, Wuhan, Shanghai, Dongguan, 
Chengdu), with ongoing capacity expansion in Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to AVC given its leading position in 
thermal/cooling component and total solution strategy to 
ride on strong AI market demand, double-digit sales/EPS 
CAGR in 2026-28 with rising liquid cooling sales mix and 
ASP/margin increase.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Asia Vital Components (AVMPF) 
Our PO of NT$3,650 is based on 23x 2027E EPS. We use 23x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since 2020) in view of AVC's double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. We 
believe AVC's leading position in thermal/cooling component market and strong AI 
market demand will drive rising liquid cooling sales mix and ASP/margin increase.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) stronger demand in AI server, server, PC, smartphone, (2) 
better operating leverage with larger revenue scale, (3) less intensive competition, (4) 
faster product certification, (5) faster capacity expansion, (6) favorable FX movement 
(USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to help on GM and/or non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/component 
shortage, (2) weaker server/PC/smartphone market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) GM pressure due to higher material/labor/utility cost, (4) more 
intensive competition, (5) delayed product certification, (6) slower capacity expansion, 
(7) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-
operating items.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
A Closer Look at the Fundamentals
Why we changed our minds – March caution, May upside
surprise
We downgraded Winbond and Nanya Tech in March (Old Memory: Turning Selective) as
elevated DDR4 pricing encouraged supply responses and customer pushback, pointing to
narrower shortages and moderating price hikes. In May, we reversed those calls as lower
expected supply from Micron and Hynix, alongside stronger server and enterprise SSD

demand, widened the projected supply-demand gap (Old Memory: Upside Surprise
Ahead). The distinction matters: March was a selective DDR4 call; today, we see a broader
challenge to old memory’s pricing durability.
What has changed
Pricing power cannot offset weaker demand indefinitely
Our constructive thesis was that niche memory, with limited fixed-price LTA constraints,
could reprice faster and outperform mainstream memory. We now see that flexibility

becoming a two-way risk. Rising uncertainty over cloud spending weakens confidence in
incremental enterprise demand, while much weaker consumer demand challenges
customers’ ability to absorb further hikes.
After the US midterms, our US team now expects ~20% of 2028e US
data center capacity additions to shift to 2029
This include some projects in Texas and Ohio, as governors introduce new data center
legislation (refer to Buy the Drop: AI Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms by

Stephen Byrd). Stephen has shifted ~5GW of US data center capacity additions from 2028
to 2029  ( Exhibit 11 ) – equivalent to 20% of US and 10% of global 2028 capacity
additions. This implies a 4% Y/Y decline in US data center GW additions  in 2028 before a
38% Y/Y rise in 2029. We now expect global data center additions to increase 9% in 2028
and    20% Y/Y  in 2029.
This could create an air pocket across the tech supply chain in 2028,
potentially reducing pricing power and leading to an inventory build

Our latest PC semis work highlights procurement pull-ins in 1Q–3Q26, followed by a
deeper correction in 4Q, reinforcing the risk that earlier orders overstated underlying
consumption (PC Semis: More Divergent Outlooks).
Supply-demand update –  looking beyond the shortage
Our revised models for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, and DDR4 point to progressively
more balanced markets, particularly into 2028. We now expect 2028 supply-demand
deficits of -3%, -5%, and -1% for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, with a +1% excess for DDR4.

Near-term tightness need not disappear immediately for stocks to de-rate: a narrowing
shortage can reduce incremental pricing power before earnings peak. We therefore place

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
less weight on extrapolating recent ASP gains and more on normalized demand and
supply responses and sustainable profitability.

Winbond: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We adjust our EPS estimates +8% for 2026, -9% for 2027, and -22% for 2028:  The
2026 EPS increase reflects 3Q26 actual results. We also factor in a potential peak in
pricing power across NOR, SLC, and DDR4. Compared to the previous estimate, we now
reduce our flash wafer ASP assumptions throughout 2027-2028 and  26% into 4Q28, and
reduce our DRAM ASP per bit estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  23% into 4Q28,

without changing our flash or DRAM shipment estimates. Apart from reduced revenue
estimates, we also lower our gross margin estimates to account for the reduced ASP
estimates. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in
our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these
changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potentially delays in 2028 US cloud capex.

Winbond: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to  NT$120
We continue to apply P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, in line with our
approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in view of the industry's high earnings
volatility. Our price target (base case scenario value) is set at 1.2x 2027e BVPS vs. our
prior target multiple of 2.9x 2027e BVPS, to reflect the potential peak in pricing power.
Our 1.2x target P/B is above our 1.0x P/B valuation anchor –  we still favor the company's

CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for their long-term potential. We see 1.0x P/B as the
downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 58% is almost entirely a function  of reduced target
multiple, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declines only 2% in our new estimate.

Our bull case value is reduced from NT$342 to NT$180, and our bear case value is
reduced from NT$118 to NT$50, based on 1.8x and 0.5x 2027e BVPS, respectively
(previously 3.4x and 1.2x).
We downgrade Winbond to EW
We continue to like the CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for longer-term potential, but we
see near-term pressure from NOR, SLC NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud

capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We believe the stock still has strong
valuation support at 1.0-1.2x P/B, and therefore we believe an EW rating is fair.

GigaDevice: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We cut our EPS estimates  25% for 2026, 36% for 2027, and 33% for 2028:  The 2026e
EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR4, NOR Flash, and SLC NAND.
We now reduce our DRAM and others revenue estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 43%
into 4Q28, mainly reflecting changes in our DDR4 ASP estimates. We also reduce our

Flash (including NOR and SLC NAND) ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  25% into
4Q28, without changing our Flash shipment estimates. We did not materially change our
GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

GigaDevice: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target  to Rmb259
We continue to derive our price target, which is our base case value, from a residual
income model. We believe this best captures the stock’s long-term value.
Our base case value cut of 65% can be attributed ~33% to earnings estimate revisions, as
has been elaborated, and ~33% from multiple compression, driven by intermediate growth
rate cut and beta increase.  (Note: the overall price target change is calculated from the

fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates are illustrative and should
not be added together or compounded to derive the overall change.).
We cut our intermediate growth rate to 8.0% from 11.3% to reflect stronger changes in
the supply environment compared to the end market demand over the medium to long
term. The potential peak in pricing power for niche memory (DDR4, SLC, MLC, NOR) and
the potential delay of 2028 US cloud capex now suggest slower revenue growth for

GigaDevice from 2026 to 2036. We think a 3.3ppt p.a. growth rate cut reflects the new
supply/demand dynamics with a closer gap.
We also raise our beta to 1.50 from 1.14, leading our cost of equity assumption to rise to
11.8% from 9.4%. This  reflects a cautious valuation scenario, to account for the recent
memory segment volatility, 70%+ correction from the stock price peak in June, and the
fabless model, which does not provide a floor support for valuation similar to the IDM

model. The elevated beta reflects the company's exposure to rapidly changing niche
memory pricing dynamics that could severely affect earnings with a downward bias
compared to our financial forecasts.
Our other key assumptions remain unchanged, including equity risk premium of 6.5%, risk-
free rate of 2% and payout ratio of 40%.
Our bull case value falls to Rmb448 from Rmb1,300, and our bear case value to Rmb112
from Rmb324.
We downgrade GigaDevice to EW

We still see the company as a domestic leader in MCU localization and believe its
customized DRAM business could show a revenue ramp over the next 1-2 years. We also
still like its strategic partnership with CXMT, which provides both capacity support and
process tech improvements. However, we see near-term pressure from the NOR, SLC
NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which industry supply is ramping faster than
demand increase, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to

2029. Fabless design houses do not face depreciation and utilization issues during a down-
cycle, but at the same time they lack a similar valuation anchor as IDM/foundry players
from their book value, and therefore we see potentially continued high volatility ahead.
Our new price target implies a 2027e P/E of 9x, below the historical average of 38x since
2016, owing to changes in pricing power.

PSMC: Earnings Estimate     Revisions
We    adjust our EPS forecasts +10% for 2026 and -11% each for 2027 and 2028:      The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e
EPS changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR3/3 and SLC NAND. We
now reduce our DRAM wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 19% into 4Q28,
without changing our DRAM wafer shipment estimates. We did not materially change our

GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

PSMC: Valuation     Methodology
Our price target  is cut to NT$54
We incorporate our revised estimates and maintain our P/B valuation methodology. Our
new target, which is our base case value,  is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down from 2.0x
previously –  below the historical average of 1.5x since 2021, to reflect pricing
uncertainties.
Our base case value/price target cut of 51% is almost entirely a result of target multiple
reduction, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declined only 1.5% in our new

estimate. For PSMC, we use 1.0x P/B as the downside valuation anchor –  book value
provides a psychological support level for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during
cyclical downturns. When earnings are temporarily depressed, investors typically place
greater weight on the underlying net asset base.
We decrease our bull case value from NT$177 to NT$86 and our bear case value from NT
$55 to NT$27, implying 1.6x and 0.5x 2027e P/B, respectively.

We downgrade PSMC to EW
We are positive on the company's emerging 3D AI foundry business, and we believe PSMC
is well positioned to benefit from its Micron partnership, including front-end migration to
the one-year  process node and back-end opportunities in advanced DRAM production
(e.g., HBM). The company is also catching up with the DRAM pricing up-cycle.
At the same time, its logic foundry business is set to enjoy continued price hikes into 2027.

However, on its memory foundry side, we see near-term pressure from DDR3/4, in which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud
capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029.
We believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we
believe an EW rating is fair.

Macronix: Earnings  Estimate Revisions
We cut our 2026, 2027, and 2028 EPS forecasts 14%, 20%, and 21%, respectively: The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across NOR and MLC/SLC NAND. We
now reduce our NAND and /NOR wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 15%
and 8% into 4Q28 respectively, without changing our wafer shipment estimates. We did

not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are
largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a
good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating
price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Macronix: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$90 from NT$220
We continue to use a P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, which is our
base case value, in line with our approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in
view of the industry's high volatility. Our new target is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down
from 2.1x previously – below the average of 1.5x since 2017, given the potential peak in
pricing power for NOR and SLC/MLC NAND. For Macronix, we now use 1.0x P/B as the

downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 59% reflects both target multiple reduction and a
15% 2027 BVPS estimate reduction (relatively large because of a smaller base).
We cut our bull case value from NT$355 to NT$145, which is 1.6x our 2027e BVPS. We cut

our bear case value from NT$100 to NT$41, which is 0.5x our 2027e BVPS.
We downgrade Macronix to EW
We continue to like its unique position in MLC NAND as the only major supplier still
ramping up capacity. However, we do see near-term pressure from NOR, DDR3/4, and
even SLC/MLC NAND, in all of which  industry supply is ramping faster than demand is
increasing, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We
believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we believe

an EW rating is fair.

Nanya Tech: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
Our 2026 estimates are unchanged;  we cut our 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates  7% and
9%,respectively: We factor in a potential peak in DDR3/4 pricing power, which affects
Nanya Tech's top line and margins. We now reduce our Nanya Tech DRAM ASP per bit
estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  6% into 4Q28, without changing our wafer
shipment estimates. We did not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in

net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross
profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these changes now as a result of the
narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Nanya Tech: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$600 to NT$550
Our price target (base case scenario value) reflects a target BVPS multiple of 2.04x for
2027 (was 4.47x) versus ROE of 59% for 2027.  We lower our target multiple to reflect
potential peak in DDR4 pricing power and recent memory segment volatility. For Nanya
Tech, our new base case target P/B multiple of 2.04x P/B is stronger than our downside

valuation anchor of 1.0x P/B for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks.  This is because we
expect Nanya to remain relatively resilient compared to other DDR4 players in our
coverage, given stronger process technology and potential DDR5 migration.
Our base case value/price target cut of 8% reflects both target multiple reduction and
BVPS estimate adjustment of 3% arising from earnings estimate revisions.
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$1,270 and NT$330 to NT$1,150 and NT

$300, respectively -- 4.27x and 1.12x 2027e BVPS.
We remain OW
Despite potentially peaking pricing power seen in DDR4, we remain positive on DDR5 in
view of continued strong demand from servers. Nanya is already producing DDR5 on its
second-generation 10-nm process, with DDR5 contributing ~10% of revenue; its next
process enters customer qualification in 1Q27, and EUV implementation is targeted for
2028. This establishes its edge in process technology vs the other DDR4 vendors in our

coverage and a stronger position in DDR5 migration. Valuation is also attractive, in our
view. Therefore, we remain OW on Nanya Tech.

AP Memory: Estimate revisions summary
We  adjust our EPS estimates  +21% for 2026, +2% for 2027, and -12% for 2028: We
factor in 3Q26 preliminary results. We adjust our 2028 forecasts  mainly to account for
supply chain risks. We note that the company's IoTRAM is less affected by  volatility of
typical commodity DRAM pricing. The 2027e and 2028e EPS changes mainly reflect
supply chain uncertainties around S-SiCap. We now reduce our S-SiCap unit shipment

estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  50%+ into 4Q28. We also slightly reduced  our IoT
RAM ASP estimates throughout 2027-28 to reflect peaking DDR3/4 pricing power. In
addition, we reduced our GM assumptions across product lines to reflect potential  peak in
pricing power and supply chain uncertainties. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates
are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is
a good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating

price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Phison: Estimate Revisions
We adjust our 2026, 2027, and 2028 EPS estimates: We factor in 3Q26 results, which
demonstrated continued revenue growth (+27% Q/Q) but also gross margin decline
(59.7% in 3Q, down from 65.3% in 2Q). In 4Q26 and 2027, we expect emerging weakness
from consumer tech and a more moderate pricing environment to result in 4Q revenue
decline, with GM starting to normalize. We think normalized GM is likely to extend into

2028.
We present further discussion of our EPS estimates under Valuation Methodology.

Phison: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$2,588 to NT$1,357
Our price target is our base case value, derived from a residual income (RI) model. Our cut
reflects our latest earnings estimates. We also cut our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.0% to 3.0% to be broadly in line with a conservative estimate of long-term
nominal economic growth in developed economies. We believe this is appropriate for

Phison because rising storage demand should support continued growth, while declining
NAND prices per bit, competition, and maturing SSD penetration should limit the
conversion of volume growth into earnings growth, bringing its growth closer to that of
the broader economy over time.
Our other RI parameters are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.0%, a medium-
term growth rate of 9.0%, and a dividend payout ratio assumption of 50.0%.
Apart from the earnings estimate revisions and terminal growth rate change, a material

part of the price target change is driven by an error in the previously published residual
income model (August 2026). The error has now been corrected. In the previous RI model,
the 2028e net income was mistakenly calculated as 2027e net income * (1+ intermediate
growth rate), where it should have been linked to the actual 2028e net income forecast.
This overstated the previous price target.
Our earnings estimates have also changed materially  from the previously published

version: they increase 12% for 2026, 60% for 2027, and 37% for  2028. This mainly reflects
higher GM assumptions of 61.2%, 51.9%, and 42.0%, respectively; previously we assumed
60.1%, 43.4%, and 39.5%.
Our base case value/price target is lowered 48%, of which roughly +37% is contributed by
earnings estimate revisions, -6% is contributed by terminal growth rate reduction, and -
83% is contributed by the correction of the prior RI model error. (The overall price target

change is calculated from the fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates
are illustrative and should not be added together or compounded to derive the overall
change.)
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$3,400 and NT$1,250 to NT$1,782 and NT
$655, respectively, implying 8.4x 2027e EPS in the bull case and 3.1x 2027e EPS in the
bear case.
We downgrade Phison to UW
The company hopes its AiDaptive module and service business can pick up and offset the

margin pressure in the conventional module business in the coming quarters, which we
believe is worth monitoring. However, its 3Q GM  of 59% missed the Street estimate of
60.3%; we see this as a potential sign that the previously strong margin profile supported
by low-cost inventory may start to face more downside. Specifically, the AI storage part of
its business (eSSD, and cSSD for AI PC, together 40% of revenue) may face margin
decline. This is what we have been cautious about, and we see the 3Q margin miss as

having provided a good opportunity to downgrade this stock, which we view as

### Page 46

M
Idea
46
overvalued based on our revised assumptions.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3090.TT 日電貿 
 庫存等待進一步消化，日正當中再續衝

[財務資訊更新，法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
日電貿成立於 1993 年，為台灣規模最大的被動元件代理商，其代理的企業品牌涵蓋
美國、日本與韓國等多家知名被動元件與電子零組件公司，產品線以各種電容、電感、
半導體元件與其他光電元件等為主，近期則開始聚焦於高規格電容類產品。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 172 
【前次投資建議】強力買進，256 
【大盤指數/股價】43092 / 132.5

➢ 因業外減少，3Q26 EPS 降至 2.46 元，低於投研部預期 
3Q26 營收 58.61 億元，QoQ+13.7%、YoY+44.7%；受惠 AI 伺服器及
資料中心需求強勁，帶動 MLCC 等高價值產品出貨成長，毛利率提升至
21.12%，QoQ+3ppts，YoY+5.2ppts；EPS 2.49 元，獲利呈現季衰退
並低於投研部預期，主因為 2Q26 存在一筆業外股利收入 4.5 億元。 
➢ 4Q26 客戶放緩拉貨節奏，且大客戶佔比上升，營收與獲利續降 
受惠 AI 高階 MLCC 供需緊俏，以及日韓原廠產能轉移，部分消費性產
品交期已延長至 24～36 週，預期 4Q26 AI 與一般型產品報價同步調漲，
MLCC 營收占比可望由 52%升至 54%。預估 4Q26 營收 58.17 億元，
QoQ-0.8% 、 YoY+52.5% ；毛利率 20.64% ， QoQ-0.5ppts ，
YoY+2.7ppts；EPS 2.40 元。惟 2026 年底客戶控管庫存，加上大型客
戶議價能力較強，獲利率略受稀釋。 
➢ 供需失衡延續至 2027 年，漲價潮與 AI 占比破六成將使獲利再升 
日電貿因輝達 Vera Rubin 平台出貨遞延，客戶拉貨速度低於預期，導致
3Q26 存貨大幅攀升至 46.03 億元，主要反映高階元件 ASP 上升及長交
期產品備貨需求，惟隨 2027 年初 Rubin 放量，庫存可望逐步去化。展
望 2027 年，AI 與傳統伺服器需求同步成長，高階 MLCC 因製程難度
高、良率偏低，供給持續緊俏，預估 MLCC 營收占比升至 57%，年增
49.7%；固態電容及電解電容營收亦分別成長 44.7%及 34.9%。隨著 AI
營收占比提升至約 60%，加上原廠漲價及產品組合優化，毛利率有望重
拾上漲動能。 
➢ 預估 2027 年 EPS 14.37 元，給予買進評等，目標價 172 元 
投研部預估日電貿於 2026 年/2027 年營收 213.24 億元/304.43 億元，
YoY+35.58%/+42.76% ； 毛 利 率 19.99%/22.15% ， YoY+3.6ppts 
/2.2ppts；稅後 EPS 9.80 元/14.37 元，目前 PER 約在 13.5X/9.2X，參
考過去五年 PER 區間大部分在 10X~18X，目前股價以 2027 年衡量低
於區間下緣，考量公司獨家代理的多項高階產品因供需持續失衡而有望
漲價，使 AI 佔比持續上升，故給予買進評等，但下修目標價至 172 元
(2027F PE 12X)。

個股報告

3Q26 因業外減少，EPS 降至 2.49 元，低於投研部預期 
日電貿 3Q26 營收 58.61 億元，QoQ+13.7%、YoY+44.7%，主因為 AI 伺服器、資
料中心及電源相關需求同步增加，帶動陶瓷電容(MLCC)等高附加價值產品出貨成長，
以營收結構來看，公司三大類別分別為 MLCC 佔 52%、固態電容(SCP)佔 26%、電解
電容(ECP)佔 9%。此外，SCP 佔比下滑至 26%，因客戶擔憂缺貨而於 2Q26 提前備
貨，加上 3Q26 MLCC 成長速度更快所致；毛利率提升至 21.12%，QoQ+3ppts，
YoY+5.2ppts，呈現出 AI 伺服器與資料中心需求強勁，帶動 MLCC 與各類電容產品
出貨上升的現象；然而稅前淨利 8.98 億元，QoQ-13.3%，主因為 2Q26 存在一筆業
外股利收入 4.5 億元；EPS 2.49 元，QoQ-22.4%，YoY+26.4%。3Q26 營收低於投
研部預期，因公司客戶於 2Q26 提前備貨，故 3Q26 季增幅回歸較穩定狀態；獲利亦
因上述的業外減少而低於預期。累計前三季 EPS 7.4 元，YoY+76.2%。 
圖 1：日電貿產品組合佔比預估

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
2026 年底客戶放緩拉貨節奏，且大客戶佔比上升，4Q26 營收與獲利續降 
日電貿目前因 AI 用的高階 MLCC 供需緊俏，一般型產品也受到日韓原廠產能轉移影
響，部分消費性產品交期已拉長至 24~36 週，預期 4Q26 公司旗下 AI 與一般型料號
報價將調漲，帶動相關營收隨之上升，因此 MLCC 比重有望由 52%進一步升至 54%。
投研部預估 4Q26 營收 58.17 億元，QoQ-0.8%，YoY+52.5%，季衰退的原因推測為
公司客戶於 2026 年底進行盤點並控管庫存，故傾向放緩拉貨腳步；毛利率 20.64%，
QoQ-0.5ppts，YoY+2.7ppts，儘管 AI 占比將超過 50%，但大型客戶占比也持續增
加，公司對這些客戶的產品議價與通路利潤率相對較低，故毛利率有所稀釋；營業利
益率 13.97%，QoQ-0.85ppts，YoY+3.2ppts；稅後 EPS 2.40 元，QoQ-3.5%，
YoY+89.3%。 
MLCC 供需失衡延續至 2027 年，漲價潮與 AI 占比破六成將使獲利再升 
目前輝達(NVDA.US) Vera Rubin 平台出貨遞延，使日電貿的客戶拉貨速度低於預期，
因此在 3Q26 季底累積的庫存金額高達 46.03 億元，且高階元件 ASP 上升，再加上
這些產品的交期較長而需建立更安全的庫存水位，因此相較過往才會異常飆高。公司
認為未來一至兩季需求仍佳，待 Rubin 於 2027 年初開始大量拉貨後，庫存水位可望
下降並轉化為營收，同時存貨週轉天數也從目前的 86 天逐季下降。 
過去兩年，AI 伺服器市場快速成長，傳統伺服器需求則相對溫和；然而，隨著企業 IT

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
支出回溫與 CSP 大廠持續擴大資本支出，預期自 2026 年~2027 年，傳統伺服器與
AI 伺服器將同步成長，帶動整體伺服器產業規模持續擴大。就市場規模而言，全球伺
服器於 2027 年預估約達 1.1 兆美元，並於 2028 年再提升至 1.6 兆美元，年增幅約
15%。其中，輝達 NVL72 機櫃將成為重要成長動能，2028 年相關市場產值可達約 1
兆美元，占整體市場約 63%；另一方面，ASIC 伺服器近年出貨量快速增加，預估至
2028 年市場滲透率將提升至 65%，顯示雲端業者正逐步擴大自研晶片的部署。從晶
片出貨量來看，2025 年 ASIC 約為 430 萬顆，輝達與 AMD 相關 GPU 則約 550 萬
顆；至 2028 年，ASIC 出貨量預估將增至 2,220 萬顆，較 2025 年成長約 5.2 倍，
GPU 相關出貨量則可達 1,610 萬顆，成長約 2.9 倍。整體而言，未來伺服器市場將由
傳統伺服器復甦、AI 訓練與推論需求擴張，以及 ASIC 自研晶片加速滲透等多重因素
共同推動，顯示 GPU 與 ASIC 兩大運算架構將呈現同步成長，而非單純的替代關係。 
圖 2：日電貿存貨金額 單位：億元

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
AI 伺服器所需的高階 MLCC，因產品規格涉及高容值、高耐壓與高可靠度要求，製程
難度明顯高於一般規格產品，導致產品良率相對偏低。相較一般 MLCC 良率可達
99.9%，部分 AI 高階產品良率依產品別與不同客戶的設計差異約落在 40%~80%不
等，使生產相同數量的合格品，需投入更多製造資源，因此形成較大的產能消耗。儘
管如此，受惠 AI 伺服器與高效能運算需求持續擴張，高階 MLCC 訂單仍相當充足，
供給短期內不易快速增加。日電貿目前客戶需求都維持強勁，尤其 MLCC 產品拉貨動
能明確，因此維持緊俏的狀況將延續至 2027 年，使其成為被動元件產業中具備較高
成長潛力的重要產品。投研部預估 MLCC 佔比將自 2026 年底的 54%上升至 2027 年
的 57%，貢獻營收 YoY+49.7%；固態電容與電解電容雖然分別由 25%下滑至 24%與
9%下滑至 8%，但以貢獻營收衡量，YoY 仍分別增長 44.7%與 34.91%。儘管通路商
的毛利率較其他被動元件製造廠的毛利低，但在 2027 年預期 AI 占比將可望達 60%，
因此仍有望藉由調整旗下客戶去優化產品組合，且代理的原廠漲價也有機會帶動通路
價差增加，故 2027 年毛利率有望再次上揚。 
預估 2027 年 EPS 14.37 元，給予買進評等，目標價 172 元 
投 研 部 預 估 日 電 貿 2026 年 /2027 年 營 收 213.24 億元/304.43 億 元 ，
YoY+35.58%/+42.76%；毛利率為 19.99%/22.15%，YoY+3.6ppts/2.2ppts；稅後
EPS 9.80 元/14.37 元，目前 PER 約在 13.7X/9.2X，參考過去五年 PER 區間大部分在
10X~18X，目前股價以 2027 年衡量低於區間下緣，考量公司獨家代理的多項高階產
品因供需持續失衡而有望漲價，使 AI 佔比持續上升，故給予買進評等，但下修目標價
至 172 元(2027F PE 12X)。

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500 원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 비현금성 비용 항목 개선으로 시장 기대치
를 상회했으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인플레이션 등으로 연간 가이던스는 
하향되었습니다. 올해 생산 차질에 따라 이연된 신차 판매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시화하는 부분에 주목 필요합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제외하면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년으로 이연된 것이라는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본격화된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로컬업체들의 공세를 방어하는 모습을 
확인하는 것이 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 노후차종 중심 운영에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근원적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(YoY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(YoY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(YoY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(YoY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(YoY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(YoY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

현대차 (005380)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH

Document 313: Anthropic IPO前夕，OpenAI推出最强模型GPT-6.2 Astra，称性能超越Fable 5.2

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO前夕，OpenAI推出最强模型GPT-6.2 Astra，称性能超越Fable 5.2.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

OpenAI 在 Anthropic 上市前⼣ " 精准狙击 " ，推出 GPT-6.2 Astra 与 Sol 双模型。 GPT-6.2
Astra 定位于复杂专业⼯作场景，涵盖⾦融建模与科学研究等领域，在多步骤专业⼯作基准
测试中的得分较 Claude Fable 5.2 ⾼出 5.5 分，⽽成本仅为后者的 5% 。 GPT-6.2 Sol ⾯向
⽇常任务，成本较 Astra 进⼀步⼤幅降低。
摘要：
OpenAI 推出旗舰模型最新迭代版本，在性能提升的同时⼤幅降低使⽤成本，并在多项基准测试
中超越竞争对⼿ Anthropic 的顶级模型。
此次发布的 GPT-6.2 Astra 定位于复杂专业⼯作场景，涵盖⾦融建模与科学研究等领域，同步推
出的 GPT-6.2 Sol 则⾯向⽇常任务。
OpenAI 表⽰，GPT-6.2 Astra 在多步骤专业⼯作基准测试中的得分较竞品 Claude Fable 5.2 ⾼
出 5.5 分，⽽成本仅为后者的 5% 。此次发布恰逢 Anthropic 备受市场关注的上市前⼣，时间节点

颇为微妙。
新模型的推出同时⾯临外界对前沿 AI 系统安全⻛险的持续审视。 OpenAI 曾于 9 ⽉底以模型 " 未能
充分限定⼯作范围与权限边界 " 为由，暂停发布 GPT-6.1 Astra ；此前，该公司还曾短暂中⽌对最
⾼级 AI 系统的训练，并在切断模型的互联⽹访问权限后⽅才重启。
安全顾虑促使发布推迟近两个⽉
GPT-6.2 Astra 的发布距上⼀版本被叫停已近两个⽉。 OpenAI 在 9 ⽉底表⽰， GPT-6.1 Astra
在 " 限定⼯作范围与授权边界，以及向⽤⼾反馈已完成⼯作类型 " ⽅⾯未达预期，因此决定搁置发
布。
在更早前， OpenAI 曾短暂暂停对其最⾼能⼒ AI 系统的训练。这⼀决定源于⼀系列安全事件 ——
测试版模型曾在未获授权的情况下访问第三⽅在线平台。 OpenAI 在切断相关模型的互联⽹访问
权限后，才于近期恢复训练进程。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Anthropic 同样⾯临类似的安全挑战。两家领先 AI 初创公司此前均表⽰，鉴于接连发⽣的安全事
件，放缓开发节奏具有合理性。
性能提升， API 价格下调 20%
在技术层⾯， GPT-6.2 Astra 在多项关键基准测试中刷新纪录。在测试多步骤专业⼯作能⼒
的 "Agents' Last Exam" 中，该模型得分较上⼀代 GPT-6 Astra ⾼出 5.2 分，成本不及后者的三
分之⼀。
在科学⼯作能⼒测试 "Terminal-Bench-Science 0.1" 中，得分同样较前代⾼出 9 个百分点，成本同
样不及三分之⼀。
API 定价⽅⾯， GPT-6.2 Astra 的输⼊与输出 token 价格均较 GPT-6 Astra 下调 20%，缓存输⼊则
享有 60% 折扣，每百万 token 报价分别为：输⼊ 8 美元、缓存输⼊ 0.40 美元、输出 40 美元。
同步发布的 GPT-6.2 Sol 定位为⽇常⼯作模型， API token 价格维持与 GPT-6.1 Sol ⼀致。

在代码编写基准测试 "FrontierCode 2.0" 中， GPT-6.2 Sol 的得分与 GPT-6.2 Astra 相差不⾜ 0.5
分，成本仅为后者的约五分之⼀。

### Page 3

在⽇常任务评估基准 "LifeVal" 中， GPT-6.2 Sol 的得分超越 GPT-6 Astra ，⽽成本约为后者的七
分之⼀。
专业⽂档输出更贴近⾏业标准
OpenAI 将本次发布的⼀⼤重点落在专业⽂档质量上。
公司表⽰，GPT-6.2 Astra ⽣成的幻灯⽚、电⼦表格及⽂档能更严格遵循⽤⼾指令与⾏业惯
例， "AI 感 " 明显降低，写作⻛格更加直接、具体，减少了泛泛⽽论的总结性语句和冗余内容。
在法律⽂书评估基准 "Harvey LAB-AA v1.1" 中，GPT-6.2 Astra 的通过率超过 Claude Fable 5.2
逾⼀倍，成本约为后者的六分之⼀。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
在涵盖⾦融、数据科学、⽣物技术和市场营销等领域真实项⽬的内部基准 "GDPval v2" 中， GPT-
6.2 Astra 较上代提升 6.2 个百分点，同时输出 token 减少 22% ； GPT-6.2 Sol 较上代 GPT-6.1 Sol 提
升 5.3 个百分点。
竞争压⼒下的估值与上市博弈
此次发布的时机具有明显的竞争意味。 OpenAI 选择在 Anthropic 即将登陆华尔街前推出新模型，
两家公司正围绕企业客⼾展开激烈争夺。
与此同时， OpenAI ⾃⾝也处于关键的资本运作节点。据悉，该公司⽬前正在进⾏新⼀轮融资，
估值达 1.25 万亿美元，并已将上市计划推迟⾄明年。
在融资窗⼝临近之际，以更低成本提供更强性能，或将成为 OpenAI 向潜在机构投资者展⽰商业
竞争⼒的重要⽀撑。

### Page 5

市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 371: Fed's Hawks Are Squawking

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fed's Hawks Are Squawking.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Fed's Hawks Are Squawking
Date:    9 hours ago
Fed's Hawks Are Squawking

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Fed's Hawks Are Squawking
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 18 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Jiri Plistil / Unsplash

I. Markets In An AI Fog
Stocks were mixed in a lackluster session today as investors navigated
the fog around the AI trade and the Fed's next move. The S&P 500
slipped 0.2%, the DJIA fell 1.1%, the Nasdaq gained 0.4%, and the
Russell 2000 rose 0.5% (chart).

### Page 2

Nvidia's shares declined 2.0% today despite reporting impressive Q3
earnings growth and upbeat Q4 and FY 2027 guidance (charts). The stock
seems cheap at a forward multiple of 11.0. The S&P 500 Semiconductor
industry trades at nearly the identical forward P/E, of 11.1, representing a
significant discount to the S&P 500's valuation multiple of 17.6.
Investors are focusing on Nvidia's more rigorous and disciplined approach
to financing the AI boom and uncertainty over who will ultimately succeed

Jensen Huang. They also still want to know how much of Q3's revenues
were attributable to China's purchases of banned Nvidia chips. Anthropic's
Thursday IPO will provide the next key catalyst for the AI trade.

### Page 3

Bearish oil fundamentals continue to wrestle with the geopolitical risk
premium embedded in crude prices. The Energy Information
Administration’s report today showed US crude inventories rising 3.4
million barrels in the week ended November 13. However, the Middle East
conflict isn't over and could still re-escalate. Fundamentals won the
market’s tug-of-war today, with the price of West Texas Intermediate crude
falling 3.2% to $83.26 a barrel. As a result, the 10-year Treasury yield

eased 4bps to 5.07% (chart).
II. The Fed's Warsh Problem
At its October meeting, the FOMC voted 10-2 to hold the federal funds
rate (FFR) steady rather than hike it by 25bps. The October FOMC
meeting minutes, released today, indicated that policymakers wanted

### Page 4

more time to assess the AI investment cycle, the effects of September's
rate hike, and geopolitical developments. Most FOMC participants
acknowledged that inflation remained troublesome, while some warned
that further delays could necessitate more disruptive tightening later.
Since October, economic growth has remained strong, the labor market
has improved, and inflation pressures have mounted. The incoming data
increasingly suggest that the Fed should have hiked in October. The FFR

futures market now anticipates two 25bps rate hikes over the next six
months (chart).
As a result, Fed officials are increasingly calling for greater urgency in
tightening monetary policy. Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan warned that
rates may need to rise faster, while St. Louis Fed President Alberto
Musalem sees several hikes before spring. Governor Christopher Waller
argued Wednesday that the Fed must reverse all three of last year's
25bps cuts and “may need to do more.”

Waller outlined two options: (1) hike 50bps in December or (2) hike 25bps
while signaling another increase in January. But Chairman Kevin Warsh is
averse to forward guidance, which nixes the second option and may leave
the Fed with no choice but to proceed with a larger hike to demonstrate
urgency.
Importantly, the October meeting’s minutes revealed that some officials
favoring a hike would have accepted a hold if the Fed had clearly

### Page 5

communicated further tightening. Warsh's communication philosophy may
thus be limiting the Fed's flexibility precisely when it needs it most.
We continue to expect a 50bps rate hike at the December meeting.
III. US Economy Still Roaring
This week's economic releases offered further evidence of an economy
that is firing on all cylinders:
(1) Retail sales. October retail sales rose 0.2% m/m, weighed down by a
1.6% decline in auto sales and a 2.3% drop in furniture sales (chart).

Underlying spending was considerably stronger, with sales excluding
autos rising 0.6% m/m. Eight of the 13 major retail categories posted
gains, led by miscellaneous store retailers (+4.7%), gasoline stations
(+1.5%), health and personal care stores (+1.5%), and nonstore retailers
(+1.2%).
Control-group retail sales, used in calculating GDP, rose a solid 0.6% m/m
in October, following an upwardly revised 0.5% gain in September (chart).

### Page 6

The latest Redbook report reinforces that positive assessment. Same-
store sales rose 9.8% y/y in the week ended November 13 (chart). With
Redbook sales growth holding near double-digit rates, there is little
evidence of a slowdown in consumer spending heading into the holiday
season.
(2) Industrial production. Tuesday's October industrial production report
showed factory activity continues to improve for the second consecutive
year after a prolonged soft patch from 2022 through 2024 (chart).

Manufacturing output rose 0.3% m/m for the second consecutive month,
even as overall industrial production fell 0.4%, largely reflecting a sharp
4.3% decline in utilities output.

### Page 7

The details suggest that the AI buildout remains a key driver of industrial
growth (chart).
Since ChatGPT's release in late 2022, technology-related industrial
production has been soaring to record highs (chart).

### Page 8

Electric and gas utilities are increasing their output to meet the needs of
data centers (chart).
Defense spending is providing another tailwind for industrial activity
(chart).

### Page 9

(3) Employment. The ADP employment report for the four weeks through
October 31 showed a solid increase of 23,750 (chart).

mid-cycle market dynamics. Last week, as the US equity 
market volatility index VIX surged above 25, we partially dialled 
up risk with a tactical idea on the more diversified US IT sector. 
Staying tactical within a diversified allocation. Equity 
volatility following last week’s Democratic clean sweep in the 
US elections has subsided. Questions about the pace of AI data 
centre buildout are likely to linger until Democrats take office in 
in January. Nvidia’s turmoil adds uncertainty. We remain 
broadly diversified and balanced in our core allocation across 
assets, sector and regions, while seeking tactical opportunities. 
History suggests sharp selloffs are followed by near-term 
volatility, particularly during macroeconomic stress. However, 
the balance of historical outcomes has typically shifted strongly 
to the upside over a three- to six-month horizon. Besides, 
historically, US equity markets have delivered the strongest 12-
month returns after opposition clean sweeps in the mid-terms. 
Rotation driving US IT sector recovery: As volatility eases, 
our opportunistic US IT equities idea, launched a week ago, has 
performed well, despite some significantly negative company 
specific developments. Attractive valuations support further 
upside. Investors are rotating within IT from semiconductors, 
affected by concerns over the pace of AI data centre buildout 
and Nvidia’s troubles, towards software and hardware 
providers. The latter have strong cash flows and balance sheets 
and are likely to benefit from AI monetisation. Together, they 
account for more than 60% of the IT sector (see page 4).

Remain constructive on US healthcare. US healthcare 
earnings are projected to grow almost 23% in 2027, the second-
fastest among MSCI US sectors. We see scope for further 
upside in the sector as innovation underpins long-term earnings 
growth. With the market rotating away from concentrated AI 
exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical demand profile 
offers a compelling exposure as a diversifier (see page 5). 
Outside the US, Switzerland and India offer diversified 
returns. The Swiss equity market consists of 41% healthcare, 
19% financial services and 14% consumer staples, providing a 
defensive alternative to investors seeking to diversify away 
from tech-heavy exposure. The Indian equity market has been 
classified as an “anti-AI” trade. Historically narrow valuation 
premiums vs. EMs, low foreign investor positioning and c. 15% 
expected 2027 earnings growth make it an attractive diversifier. 
Strong jobs and inflation reports cement December Fed 
rate hike. The US economy added 165,000 jobs in October, 
more than twice the 70,000 consensus estimate. The 
unemployment rate fell to 4.1%. Meanwhile, headline and core 
inflation in October, at 0.4% and 0.3% m/m, respectively, 
exceeded expectations as elevated fuel prices, ‘techflation’ and 
earlier tariffs fed into directly exposed sectors, including food, 
utilities and transportation services. A healthy job market keeps 
the Fed focused on lowering inflation, cementing the prospect 
of a December rate hike, potentially followed by another in Q1 
2027. A strong economy and elevated interest rates support our 
overweight allocation to US financial sector equities. 
Locking in gains from our bullish US inflation-protected 
bonds idea. While near-term price pressures remain elevated, 
we expect inflation to ease with energy costs over the coming 
year. Republicans’ heavy losses in the US mid-term elections 
increase pressure on the Trump administration to end the Iran 
conflict. One-year US inflation expectations have slumped 
alongside oil prices. A potential slowdown in the pace of AI 
buildout is also likely to ease techflation. Meanwhile, Fed rate 
hikes should further tighten financial conditions, tempering 
price pressures. This disinflationary outlook makes the risk-
reward for US inflation-protected bonds less compelling. We 
close the idea, locking in a 4.4% gain (page 6). 
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

What does history tell us about the aftermath of a single-
day sell-off of 4% or more in the S&P500 index?

Our view: The S&P500 fell 4.36% on 5 November 2026, a rare daily 
move of 3.8 standard deviations. Since 2009, there have been only

24 occasions when the index declined by 4% or more in a single

session. The immediate aftermath of such sell-offs has historically

been mixed. One-month returns ranged from around -15% to

+23%, with positive returns in 70% of cases. The weaker

outcomes were concentrated around periods of significant macro

stress, including the tail end of the 2008 Global Financial Crisis

(GFC), the 2020 COVID-19 pandemic and the 2022 monetary policy

tightening cycle. 
The historical picture becomes considerably more constructive

beyond the first month. All 24 episodes generated positive three-
month returns, ranging from roughly +0.5% to +38%, while six-
month returns were positive in 96% of cases, with gains reaching 
as much as +49%. Overall, history suggests sharp sell-offs can be 
followed by near-term volatility, particularly during periods of broader 
macro stress. However, the balance of historical outcomes has 
shifted strongly to the upside over a 3-6-month horizon. 
—  Maggie Au Yeung, Quantitative Analyst 
 o
— Francis Lim, Senior Quantitative Strategist
Where do you see the most favourable risk-reward within

the US technology sector? 
Our view: We favour high-quality Big Tech companies with 
strong balance sheets and prefer diversified exposure through 
our US Information Technology (IT) opportunistic idea. 
Rationale: The recent pullback in US semiconductor stocks has 
improved their risk-reward profile. Earnings for these companies are 
forecast to grow by 65% in 2027, the fastest rate among technology 
subsectors. Despite this outlook, recent regulatory and corporate 
governance concerns are weighing on sentiment. Potential state-
level restrictions on data centre development following the recent 
US mid-term election results could weigh on semiconductor demand 
growth. However, we believe these risks are manageable and 
continued AI adoption should support demand for AI inference. We 
prefer diversified exposure to quality semiconductor 
companies with durable demand and strong volume growth. 
To navigate volatility in the US technology sector, we favour Big Tech 
companies with strong balance sheets and resilient cash generation, 
as they are better positioned to sustain AI investments without 
relying heavily on external financing. Our US IT opportunistic idea 
combines these preferences through exposure to semiconductors, 
software and hardware, allowing investors to participate in a 
potential recovery across a broader range of earnings drivers.
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d)

Do fundamental drivers support a constructive view on the 
US healthcare sector?

Our view: We remain constructive on US healthcare, supported

by its defensive characteristics and robust earnings growth.

Rationale: US healthcare earnings are projected to grow by 22.5% 
in 2027, the second-fastest among MSCI US sectors, driven by 
improving biopharma earnings. The US healthcare sector's 
valuation gap against the broader market has been recovering from 
a steep historical discount a year ago. The sector's forward price-to-

earnings (P/E) ratio of c. 20x now exceeds the broader US market’s

c. 18.4x, but we see scope for the valuation to be supported amid

investor demand for defensive growth. The sector’s broad legislative

policy overhang has materially faded following the US election cycle,

while targeted regulatory and oversight risks remain on the watchlist.

Healthcare innovation also underpins structural growth. Wider

adoption of obesity and diabetes treatments, alongside advances in

oncology treatments, medical devices and robotic surgery, should

support long-term earnings growth. With the market rotating away

from concentrated AI exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical 
demand profile presents compelling opportunities for investors 
seeking both defensiveness and growth in the near term.
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst
What is your outlook for China’s Big Tech sector? 
Our view: We remain positive on China’s Big Tech sector, 
consistent with our Overweight stance on the country’s IT and 
communication services sector. 
Rationale: Valuations across China’s technology sector remain 
inexpensive at 14.2x, with investor attention centred on whether AI-
driven revenue can offset elevated AI-related spending, which is 
currently compressing margins. Elevated AI capex and negative 
free cash flow remain key investor concerns in the Q3 earnings 
season. Top-line momentum also remains uneven: soft domestic 
consumption continues to weigh on e-commerce, advertising and 
local services, whereas gaming and cloud computing are 
delivering resilient growth. The broader narrative is steadily 
transitioning towards commercialisation, with rapid enterprise 
penetration by AI workplace agents and upcoming AI model 
launches shifting the market focus towards active deployment and 
conversion to paid usage. Evolving regulatory frameworks 
covering AI governance, agent self-regulation, tax scrutiny and 
compliance requirements remain important variables influencing 
medium-term margin trajectories. Ultimately, valuation support, 
gradual monetisation of AI capabilities and resilient core 
segments support our Overweight positioning.
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist
US healthcare sector earnings are projected to 
grow 22.5% in 2027 – the second-fastest among 
MSCI US sectors

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Why are you closing your US Treasury Inflation-Protected

Securities (TIPS) opportunistic idea? 
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish US TIPS 
position, as the risk-reward profile has become less compelling.

The trade returned 4.4% since its initiation on 11 April 2025.

Rationale: We initiated an opportunistic bullish position in US TIPS

on 11 April 2025 as an oil and inflation hedge, expecting geopolitical

uncertainty to keep inflation expectations elevated. The risk-reward,

however, has since become less compelling, as hawkish Fed 
commentary, easing geopolitical risks and a potential slowdown in 
the pace of AI buildout are now likely to ease broader inflation and 
‘techflation’ pressures. We have therefore closed the opportunistic

idea, realising a gain of 4.4%.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
How do falling oil prices and October’s US inflation data 
affect your Fed and bond market outlook? 
Our view: Our base case envisages two further 25bps Fed hikes 
by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 2027 if energy prices 
remain consistently lower and growth trajectory normalises. We 
favour income over chasing duration gains, focusing on higher-
quality corporate bonds with 3-7-year maturities. 
Rationale: Lower oil prices have a two-sided effect: they ease 
inflationary pressures and support growth by boosting purchasing 
power and lowering business costs. Cheaper energy, therefore, 
does not automatically justify Fed easing. Our base case envisages 
two further 25bps hikes by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 
2027 if energy prices remain consistently lower. Sustained 
moderation in core inflation would challenge the near-term tightening 
path. However, October’s US consumer inflation print reinforced our 
assessment that each Fed meeting now remains a ‘live’ decision. 
With the US 10-year government bond yield already within our 5.0-
5.25% 3-month range, we favour income over chasing duration 
gains. Credit performance has diverged: Developed Market high-
yield bonds have outperformed both investment-grade credit and 
government bonds. Credit still offers attractive income, and we retain 
our preference for 3-7-year maturities, although heavy primary 
issuance remains a counterweight. We favour selective carry, with a 
preference for higher-quality corporate bonds. These include bonds 
issued by leading AI hyperscalers, where risk-reward has become 
more attractive. Our emphasis is on generating steady income, 
rather than relying on further declines in bond yields or spreads.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
While real (net-of-inflation) yields remain elevated, 
the inflation-protection function of US TIPS has 
diminished as inflation expectations ease

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d)
Does the USD’s rally have further to run?

Our view: Further upside appears limited. Stronger-than-

expected US core inflation in October and hawkish Fed

comments have pushed the US Dollar Index (DXY) above 103.

However, markets already price in more Fed tightening than we

expect. We see the DXY returning to its 101.00-102.95 range in

the near term, before gradually weakening towards our 12-

month forecast of 98.5.

Rationale: US core consumer prices rose 0.3% m/m in October,

above the 0.2% m/m expected, meeting Fed Governor Waller’s

threshold for considering a 50bps December rate hike. This pushed

US government bond yields higher, supporting the USD rally. 
However, futures now imply a Fed rate peak of 4.50-4.75% by

mid-2027, compared with our expectation of 4.25-4.50% following

two 25bps hikes, one in December and the second in H1 2027. We

expect the Fed to begin easing in H2 2027. As market expectations 
gradually converge towards our view, USD support from US bond 
yields should weaken.
Meanwhile, falling oil and petrol prices should help ease headline 
inflation in the coming months, reducing pressure for further Fed 
tightening. This reinforces our expectation of limited USD upside, 
although persistent services inflation remains a key risk.
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist
Do you expect USD/JPY to sustainably break out above 
160 in the near term?
Our view: We see USD/JPY rangebound with a downside bias, 
as growing BoJ tightening expectations and potential policy 
convergence will support gradual JPY appreciation.
Rationale: Renewed tech optimism has encouraged the re-
establishment of carry trades, providing support to the pair lately. 
Meanwhile, US core inflation beat expectations for a third 
consecutive month in October, reinforcing expectations for a 
December Fed hike. Further upside inflation surprises could widen 
the US-Japan interest rate differential and push USD/JPY higher. 
However, the risk of intervention by Japan’s Ministry of Finance 
is likely to rise materially around 160, limiting the scope for 
sustained upside. On the downside, a 25bps BoJ hike to 1.50%, 
accompanied by clear signals of consecutive hikes in March and 
June 2027, would be a significant yen-positive catalyst. A more non-
linear downside scenario would be a disorderly equity sell-off, which 
could trigger rapid JPY appreciation through carry-trade unwinding. 
We see scope for the pair to test support towards 156 in the 
coming weeks.
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist
US dollar index’s rally above 103 looks stretched

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Time to find 2027 US listed soaring stocks
Date:    4 hours ago
Time to find 2027 US listed soaring stocks
View in browser
Time to ﬁnd 2027 US listed
soaring stocks
上週精選了 20 家於 2027-2028 營收及獲利增⻑將明顯優於同業的台
灣科技及半導體公司，這週精選了美國 20 家，但加⼊了 adj EPS
yoy ，⽑利及營業利潤率， rule of 40 ，及 PEG 估值⽐較
陸⾏之  ANDREW LU ON SEMI/TECHS
NOV 17 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary
上週我們提到接近年末，市場都會棄舊迎新尋找明年飆股及本夢⽐⾏情，主要

是針對營收及獲利仍有⼤幅增⻑潛⼒的公司，都會在獲利還沒爆發前提前先
跑，⽽造成  P/E 估值升級，尤其投資⼈在經歷 10-11 ⽉份的全球科技及半導體
產業股價⼤修正超過 30% 之後 ( 費半從歷史⾼點 14,655 回檔達 35% ），我們精
選了 20 家於 2027-2028 營收及獲利增⻑將明顯優於同業的台灣科技及半導體
公司（請參考之前報告：Time to ﬁnd 2027 soaring stocks），這次我們來
精選美國 20 家營收及獲利快速增⻑的科技及半導體公司，但加⼊了 adj EPS
yoy, 並⽤⽑利及營業利潤率變化來判斷營運槓桿差異，⽐較 rule of 40 預測
及⽐較未來 4 年 PEG 估值是否合理？

### Page 2

Source: Bloomberg
Top 20 US listed growing names in the sectors
因考慮量⼦電腦，⼩型核電站 SMR ，及如⾬後春筍冒出來的⼩型新興雲（⾃
Jensen 從 Nvidia 離職後，我們相信  Nvidia 對於⼩型新興雲的財務挹注會⼤
幅降低）的產業及公司獲利模式⾄今仍存有極⼤不確定性及⾵險，所以在這次
的篩選中，我們決定暫時將這三個族群的產業及相關的公司排除⽐較，但仍會
持續關注這些產業的變化。在篩選及排序彭博社分析師超過 100 家在美國市場
交易的主要科技及半導體公司  2027/2028 年度及季度營收 / 獲利預測後，我
們精選出前 20 名公司 2027 年營收及 Adj EPS 增⻑最快，獲利有機會倍增的公
司，從這些公司的⽐較表中，我們可以發現哪些公司有較⾼的營運槓桿⽑利率
及營業利潤率變化 ? 哪些公司有較⾼的 rule of 40? 哪些公司的 adj EPS P/E,
PEG 估值偏⾼？這其中包括 5 家 AI 數據算⼒中⼼及系統組裝公司， 7 家 AI 運算及

AI ⾼速互聯晶⽚公司， 3 家光纖通訊雷射光相關公司， 2 家晶⽚測試設備公司，
剩下 3 家分佈在 AI 解決⽅案，天然氣發電機，及燃料電池產業。
付費訂閱戶可分享我們精選的 20 家美國上市公司年度 /
季度營收年增排名及哪些公司的  adj EPS 轉虧為盈，
從⽑利及營業利潤率排序找哪些公司有⾼營運槓桿，⽐
較  superior rule of 40 ，哪些公司修正整理後估值仍

### Page 3

偏⾼？及分享 4 ⼤產業⾵險，參與投票，並可索取報告
圖表
Subscribed
Names with stronger sales growth
新興雲短期營收倍增可期，但⻑期成⻑⾵險待察：從 2027 年度及季度營收年
增來分析，營收年增倍增的公司主要集中在  新興 AI 數據算⼒中⼼  Neocloud
如 SpaceXSI, Nebius, 及 CoreWeave ，雖然這些公司的主要晶⽚供應商
Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang 最近離職⽽群⿓無⾸，循環融資及未來新產品技
術是否如期推出不明朗，外加這些新興雲公司要持續擴充  Nvidia GPU 機櫃算
⼒來完成營收倍增的⻑期資本開⽀有短缺⾵險，但我們認為其 3-4 年的積壓訂
單（請參考下⾯圖表）及使⽤客戶預付款來補⾜資本開⽀現⾦流出及考量其他
⼩型新興雲將提早被淘汰出局，我們認為 SpaceXSI, Nebius, 及 CoreWeave
這三家新興雲要完成 2027 年營收倍增，應該不難。
Source: The companies, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs

新興 AI 推理晶⽚，測試設備，光纖雷射營收倍增受惠：跟 AMD 在 AI 推理⽅⾯
密切合作的  Cerebras System 系統晶⽚公司，也是採⽤台積電  System on
wafer SoW 製程技術的主要客戶。 Aehr Test Systems 主要受惠於  AI
GPU/ASIC 晶⽚的晶圓級預燒與測試  (Wafer-Level Test & Burn-In, WLTBI)
設備需求。 Applied Optoelectronics 及 AXT 受惠於 AI 數據算⼒中⼼的光纖
互聯雷射光需求的爆發。 Nvidia 因 CEO Jensen Huang 離職，雖然短期 Vera

### Page 4

Rubin 需求仍⼤幅優於供給不受影響，但  AMD AI 加速器 / 機櫃及⾕歌，亞⾺
遜， Meta ， OpenAI ⾃研晶⽚競爭，主權 AI 市場（雖說主權，但很多轉租算⼒
是給中國⼤陸客戶）陸續被美國商務部要求管制，彭博社分析師仍預測 Nvidia
營收年增於 2H28 明顯減速（ vs. 75% yoy in 2027) 。
2028 營收增⻑減速？雖然彭博社分析師普遍預期美國精選 20 其 2028 年營收
增⻑會明顯減速，但我們也不排除是分析師採保守預測，我們會持續關注 AI 訂
閱及 Tokens 需求的變化， AI 代理⼈及實體世界模型  World model 應⽤的進
展，數據中⼼電⼒及資本開⽀資⾦的取得是否順利。即使 2028 年的營收增⻑
的變數增⼤，但分析師還是預期  Nebius 及  Cerebras 的營收倍增。
Source: Bloomberg, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
Adj EPS turning into positive from earlier losses

不同於台灣投資⼈沒有  non-GAAP 或調整後財報數字作為參考，美國會計準
則及分析師通常將併購後的 5-6 年商譽攤銷，⾮經常性費⽤排除在調整後⽑利
率，營業利潤率，及 EPS 之外，然後來分析其獲利變化。歸因於新興雲公司，
SoW AI 公司  Cerebras, 新興測試設備公司  Aehr 及 Cohu ，光纖通訊 InP 基
板公司  AXT 於 2026 年上半年仍多處於虧損狀態， Adj EPS 年增⽐較數字就變
得沒有意義，所以我們的 adj EPS 排序是⽐較 2028 年  adj EPS 年增的幅度，
前 5 名明顯跟 AI 運算及光纖互聯晶⽚相關。
但就從投資獲利及⾵險兩⽅來衡量，我們還是建議投資⼈以規模較⼤的⾼獲利
增⻑⿓頭公司作為核⼼持股（ SpaceXSI, Marvell, Broadcom, AMD) ，但配
合其他⾼獲利增⻑新趨勢公司作為衛星組合。

### Page 5

Source: Bloomberg, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
Emerging players have more operating leverage
從  GAAP ⽑利率及營業利潤率⽐較來看， 2027 到 2028 明顯有⾼營運槓桿三
率三升（營收增⻑，⽑利率提升，營業利潤率提升）的公司主要為台積電的
SoW AI 晶⽚由虧轉盈的主⼒客戶  Cerebras 及提供光纖通訊 EML 雷射晶⽚的
Applied Optoelectronics 。但新興雲公司如  Nebius 及 SpaceXSI 即使⽑利
率變化不⼤，靠著擴⼤營運規模且控制研發，銷售，管理費⽤，營業利潤率從
2027 到 2028 也有雙位數的增幅。這可以解釋這幾家公司為何其  adj EPS 增
幅也是名列前茅。⽽⽐較沒有營運槓桿的公司主要有⽑利率及營業利潤率已在
歷史⾼點的公司如 Nvidia ，博通， Astera Labs ，及 Credo 。
Source: Bloomberg, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs

Superior rule of 40

### Page 6

歸因於我們精選的 20 家美國上市的科技及半導體公司都是營收⾼增⻑公司，加
上⼤多數公司都有雙位數的營業利潤率（除了⾕歌主要的系統組裝公司
Celestica 只有 7% 營業利潤率外），所以其  rule of 40 指標都遠遠⾼於產業
平均， 2027 年超過 100% 的有 12 家，即使 2028 年營收年增減速，超過 100%
的仍有 5 家。
Source: Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
Valuation matters
美國精選 20 中有 7 家（ Cerebras System, Applied Optoelectronics,
SpaceXSI, Cohu, AXT, FTAI Aviation, AMD) 有機會在 2027 年的  adj EPS
倍增，⽽前 3 家  adj EPS 2026-2030 年複合增⻑率皆超過 100% （ Applied
Optoelectronics adj EPS 預測僅到 2028 ），尤其在 10-11 ⽉份科技及半導體

公司股價⼤幅拉回之後，美國精選 20 的⼤部分公司在短期來看都已回到低於
30x 2027 adj EPS 的  P/E 估值 , 雖然  Cerebras ， SpaceXSI ， Palantir ，
Bloom Energy ，  Aehr 都還交易在 30x 以上的  2027 adj EPS ，但如果⽤
2027-2030 年 4 年平均  P/E 來跟 2026-2030 年 4 年  adj EPS 複合增⻑率來⽐
較，這些公司的 4 年平均  PEG 都還是維持在 1.0 以下，這其中較為偏⾼的也只
有  Palantir 的 0.82 PEG 及 Aehr 的 0.99 PEG ⽐較接近 1 。
我們從最近美國精選 20 的股價震盪回檔來看，雖然短中期基本⾯仍然紮實，
但⻑期投資⼈已經開始考量以下 4 ⼤⾵險是否將影響⻑期營收 /adj EPS 獲利
預測及 PEG 估值被下修的⾵險。  1. Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang 離職對
Nvidia 及 AI 產業技術疊代群⿓無⾸的⻑期影響； 2. 美國⺠主黨參眾議院及州

⻑選舉⼤勝是否將嚴重放緩 AI 基礎建設，還是也想分⼀杯羹； 3. AI 數據算⼒
中⼼的基礎建設是否將被缺電，缺電網，缺資⾦⽽減緩？ 4. 很多轉租算⼒給
中國⼤陸客戶的主權 AI 市場是否被美國商務部擴⼤管制？

### Page 7

考，並⾮投資建議，個⼈應審慎評估⾵險，如因此造成任何損失，本⼈不負任

Document 491: 【國泰證期研究部】晶技(3042 TT)-初次評等買進(+20.6%)-AI光通訊升級，高頻元件量價齊揚-20261119

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】晶技(3042 TT)-初次評等買進(+20.6%)-AI光通訊升級，高頻元件量價齊揚-20261119.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 13
Status: ok

### Page 1

晶技 3042 TT  ( 電子–零組件 ) 
AI 光通訊升級，高頻元件量價齊揚 
投資與評價 
1. 晶技 3Q26 因 AI 與車用拉貨，營收創近 5 年新高；4Q26 則受惠光模組振盪
器需求延續，營運維持高檔並創歷年同期新高。 
2. 由於光模組由 800G 邁向 1.6T 帶動高頻差分振盪器出貨增加，加上車用電
子單車用量提升，晶技 26 年 AI 與車用合計營收占比由 25 年之 36%升至
44%，27 年可望突破五成，預期 26 年獲利創近 4 年新高、27 年營運創歷
史新高，預估 26 年 EPS 7.04 元、27 年 EPS 9.68 元。 
3. 目前晶技股價 128.5 元，約為 13.3X FY27E EPS，位於近 3 年本益比參考區
間 8~26X 下緣。晶技為全球石英頻率元件營收規模最大廠商，光模組時脈
元件市占率逾 20%，具備自有前段製程，將受惠光通訊規格升級與車用滲透
率提升，以參考本益比區間中間偏下之 16 倍作為評價基礎，首次給予買進
投資評等，目標價 155 元(16X FY27E PER)。

重點更新 
AI 與車用拉貨帶動，晶技 3Q26 營收創近 5 年新高 
受惠高頻差分振盪器出貨成長，800G 光模組持續量產，加上 ADAS 平台導入帶
動車用晶體及振盪器拉貨，晶技 3Q26 營收為 41.96 億元(+13.4% QoQ)，AI 與
車用產品毛利率高於公司平均，比重提升帶動毛利率上升至 35.5%(+2.4 ppts 
QoQ)，雖營業費用因持續投入高頻與 6G 同步產品研發而增加至 6.64 億元
(+10.2% QoQ)，但由於營運規模擴大，營業費用率仍較上季之 16.3%降至
15.8%，營業淨利為 8.27 億元(+32.9% QoQ)，稅後淨利 7.07 億元(+27.3% 
QoQ)，EPS 2.08 元，營收創近 5 年單季新高。

晶技 4Q26 受惠光模組振盪器需求延續，預期營運維持高檔 
晶技受惠伺服器、交換器及光模組用振盪器需求延續，出貨能見度已延伸至 2027
年，車用訂單維持滿載，平鎮廠高頻前段製程新產能亦陸續投產，抵銷手機及消
費性應用淡季影響，預估 4Q26 營收為 41.01 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，價格反映貴金
屬成本，加上 AI 與車用比重維持高檔，預估毛利率維持在與上季相近之 35.2%(-
0.3 ppts QoQ)，營業費用 6.49 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，營業淨利將季減 3.7%至 7.96
億元，營益率 19.4%(-0.3 ppts QoQ)，稅後淨利預估為 6.87 億元(-2.8% QoQ)，
EPS 2.00 元，營運維持高檔並創歷年同期新高。

報告內容 
AI 與車用拉貨帶動，晶技 3Q26 營收創近 5 年新高 
受惠光模組及交換器用高頻差分振盪器出貨成長，800G 光模組持續量產，100MHz 以上差分振
盪器出貨穩定增加，加上 ADAS 與智慧座艙平台導入帶動車用晶體及振盪器拉貨，車用訂單維持
滿載，晶技 3Q26 營收為 41.96 億元(+13.4% QoQ)，因 AI 與車用產品毛利率均高於公司平均，
比重提升帶動毛利率上升至 35.5%(+2.4 ppts QoQ)，雖營業費用因持續投入高頻與 6G 同步產品
研發而增加至 6.64 億元(+10.2% QoQ)，但由於營運規模擴大，營業費用率仍較上季之 16.3%降
至 15.8%，營業淨利為 8.27 億元(+32.9% QoQ)，營益率 19.7%(+2.9 ppts QoQ)，稅後淨利 7.07
億元(+27.3% QoQ)，EPS 2.08 元，營收創近 5 年單季新高。

晶技 4Q26 受惠光模組振盪器需求延續，預期營運維持高檔 
晶技 10 月營收 13.73 億元(-3.0% MoM，+21.9% YoY)，4Q26 手機及消費性電子進入季節性調
整，中低階成熟產品如 3225 需求相對疲弱，惟伺服器、交換器及光模組用振盪器需求延續，出貨
能見度已延伸至 2027 年，車用訂單亦維持滿載，加上平鎮廠高頻前段製程擴產設備自 4Q26 起陸
續投入營運，預估 4Q26 營收為 41.01 億元(-2.3% QoQ)；晶技於合約到期或新訂單時適度反映
貴金屬等原物料成本，加上 AI 與車用產品比重維持高檔，可抵銷新產能投產初期之折舊負擔，預
估毛利率維持在與上季相近之 35.2%(-0.3 ppts QoQ)，營業費用 6.49 億元(-2.3% QoQ)，營業淨
利將季減 3.7%至 7.96 億元，營益率 19.4%(-0.3 ppts QoQ)，稅後淨利預估為 6.87 億元(-2.8% 
QoQ)，EPS 2.00 元，營運維持高檔並創歷年同期新高。

受惠 AI 與車用占比提升，預期晶技 26 年獲利將創近 4 年新高 
晶技 1 至 10 月累計營收 126.07 億元(+13.1% YoY)，26 年營收結構由手機轉向 AI 與車用，預
估 AI 應用與車用電子營收占比將分別提升至 16%及 28%，兩者合計 44%，高於 25 年之
36%，手機占比則由 29%降至 24%，並逐步淡出中低階手機客戶，平鎮廠亦持續擴充高頻、高
精度及低相位雜訊產品之前段製程，預估 26 年營收為 153.35 億元(+14.9% YoY)，高毛利產品
比重提升帶動整體毛利率上升至 34.2%(+1.1 ppts YoY)，營業費用 24.91 億元(+4.4% YoY)，
